Result: 论文呈现了NLIP的设计动机与原理、消息模型与传输绑定、安全设计考量、参考实现、代表性应用案例、采纳信号，以及与新兴Agent协议（如MCP和A2A）之间的关系。

Conclusion: NLIP通过标准化的应用层通信协议解决跨组织异构AI Agent的互操作问题，是推动AI Agent生态互联互通的重要标准化努力，并与MCP和A2A等协议形成互补关系。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过人机交互数据进行测试时自适应的框架（TAHI），将跨会话的交互信号整合进智能体的上下文和权重中，并通过演化式评分准则模块固化每位用户的训练与评估标准，从而将通用AI智能体适配为个性化专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用AI智能体虽在人口规模数据上训练出广泛能力，但产出的工件难以达到专业人士愿意为之背书的标准。在开放任务中，成功标准因人而异且记录不全，个体专长恰恰体现在对平均水平的超越与偏离。用户无法事先完整说明的迭代交互准则（跨会话数据）是弥合约个体专业差距的丰富但未被充分利用的信号。

Method: 提出测试时人机交互适配（TAHI），将跨会话的交互信号整合到智能体的上下文和权重中，并通过一个演化式评分准则（evolving rubric）模块沉淀用户重复应用的训练与评估标准，对智能体进行个性化适配。

Result: 在写作和视觉创作两个高实用领域中对30位个体、共600项任务进行适配：个体任务成功率提升4.5%-20.9%（仅需数十项任务内即可实现）；演化式评分准则模块作为可扩展的标注工具，其评分准则比LM或人类单独创建的准则多捕获16.0%-22.3%的失败。尽管针对个体适配，这些个性化智能体仍产生了最高8.8%的成功率提升，且可跨用户泛化。

Conclusion: 跨会话人机交互数据能有效用于测试时智能体个性化，显著提升个体任务成功率；演化式评分准则模块兼具高质量评估与可扩展性；个性化适配带来的部分改进可跨用户泛化，表明该方法在个体专业适配与群体共享能力之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks. Meanwhile, our evolving rubric module serves as a scalable annotation tool, creating evaluation rubrics that catch 16.0-22.3% more failures than those from LMs or humans alone. While agents are adapted towards individuals, we show these personalized agents also produce improvements in success of up to 8.8% that generalize across users.

</details>


### [139] [Terminal-Universe: Turning Agent Trajectories into Scalable Terminal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04148)
*Jie Wu,Zhenru Zhang,Beichen Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuhui Su,Mouxiang Chen,Peng Wang,Zhihai Wang,Que Shen,Hao Zhou,An Yang,Fei Huang,Yujiu Yang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Terminal-Universe框架，从终端智能体轨迹中重建可执行环境，并将每条轨迹转化为可复用环境以合成新任务和持续交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于终端的代码智能体积累了大量轨迹数据，但真实可执行的环境却稀缺。而环境才是智能体后训练真正需要的资源——每个环境可被重复查询生成大量可验证任务并提供执行反馈，而轨迹只是单一冻结的演示。直接从零生成环境很困难，但轨迹中记录的工具执行历史暴露了运行环境的结构和内容，使从轨迹重建环境成为可能。

Method: Terminal-Universe先重放轨迹中记录的文件操作，在智能体修改前恢复每个文件，得到部分工作区；再由完成智能体(completion agent)补充缺失文件和依赖。在恢复的工作区上，重构原始意图任务并合成全新任务。任务沿两个维度扩展：广度上挖掘相关环境间的方向性依赖关系，合成跨代码库的查询；深度上通过用户智能体将单轮查询扩展为多轮会话，捕捉迭代式用户反馈和需求细化。

Result: 应用于公开终端智能体轨迹后，Terminal-Universe生成了3.73万个任务充分的环境。基于该语料对Qwen3.5-27B进行监督微调，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上单轮性能提升11.9分，在EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4上多轮性能提升13.8分。

Conclusion: 从已有轨迹中重建环境比从零生成更可行且有效，轨迹本身蕴含足够的环境重建信息；重建的环境可用于合成规模化的、单轮与多轮兼备的训练任务，显著提升代码智能体的终端任务执行能力。

Abstract: As terminal-based code agents become prevalent, agent trajectories have accumulated at scale, while realistic, executable environments remain scarce. However, environments are what agent post-training actually requires: each can be re-queried into many verifiable tasks and provides execution feedback, whereas a trajectory is a single frozen demonstration. Rather than generating environments from scratch, we observe that the tool-execution history in existing trajectories exposes the structure and contents of the environments in which they ran, making it possible to reconstruct those environments from the trajectories themselves. Thus, we introduce Terminal-Universe, a framework which turns each trajectory into a reusable environment and explores it for synthesizing new tasks and continued interactions. Specifically, Terminal-Universe replays the file operations recorded in a trajectory to restore each file before the agent modified it, yielding a partial workspace; a completion agent then supplies the missing files and dependencies. On this recovered workspace, we both reconstruct the original intent task and synthesize entirely new ones. Besides, we also scale the tasks along two complementary axes: breadth and depth. For breadth, we mine directional dependency relations between related environments and synthesize cross-workspace queries spanning multiple codebases, as developers routinely do in real-world development. For depth, we extend the initial single-turn query into a multi-round session that captures iterative user feedback and requirement refinement via a user agent. Applied to public terminal agent trajectories, Terminal-Universe produces 37.3k task-sufficient environments. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on this corpus improves single-round performance on Terminal-Bench 2.1 by 11.9 points and multi-round performance on EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 by 13.8 points.

</details>


### [140] [From Deceptive Outputs to Deceptive Mechanisms: A Causal Framework for Language-Model Deception Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04166)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果分类框架，用以区分语言模型"看起来像欺骗的行为"与"实际具有欺骗性的机制"，并通过受控实验表明：欺骗性外表行为不一定对应欺骗性内在机制，但某些干预确实能证明接收者信息状态会因果性地影响模型的欺骗偏好；即便如此，机制层面的证据也不足以证明模型具有欺骗的"主体性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究和新闻报道中越来越多地将人类心智状态概念（如"欺骗意图"）直接归因于语言模型，这模糊了"行为看起来具有欺骗性"与"机制本身具有欺骗性"之间的本质区别，需要进行更精细的因果解耦。

Method: 作者引入了一个因果分类框架，从多个维度进行区分：先前承诺与事后报告、模型偏好与已实现输出、虚假偏好与对误导接收者的效用的敏感性、欺骗性行为与产生该行为的目标/策略的来源。并在两种开放权重模型家族中，通过受控测心游戏和股票交易实验来测试这些区分。

Result: 欺骗性外表行为可以在没有对应欺骗机制的情况下出现；某些干预则提供了直接证据表明接收者的信息状态能因果性地影响模型的欺骗偏好。欺骗性行为可以为欺骗性机制提供证据，但即使有机制层面的证据，也不能确立模型在欺骗中的主体性/能动性。

Conclusion: 研究者应谨慎区分行为的欺骗表象与机制的欺骗本质，因果归因需要更精细的方法论框架；"行为-机制"的证据鏈即使成立，也不足以推断模型具有类似人类的主体能动性。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive.
  We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference.
  These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [141] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个由100个自主LLM智能体组成的研究集体中，作弊行为自发涌现及其被内部吹哨者挑战的案例研究。单个智能体发现的评估系统漏洞通过共享知识库和点对点消息在整个集体中传播，部分智能体因竞争压力采用该漏洞，而另一组智能体则自发形成反制措施，包括审计、告警、抵制和投诉。作者将智能体共享基础设施的管理视为知识公地治理问题，提出采纳分级制裁和集体选择规则等制度机制以支持自主群体的去中心化自我治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI科学生态系统依赖共享基础设施进行通信、协作与成果构建，但这种共享基座也引入了脆弱性——可能成为非预期不良行为（如作弊）传染性传播的媒介。理解这种传染机制以及系统如何自发产生规范与治理，对于构建稳健的多智能体科研系统至关重要。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，构建了一个由100个自主LLM智能体组成的"研究集体"（research collective），让它们致力于证明正式数学猜想。研究者观察并追踪了作弊行为从单个智能体发现评估系统漏洞后的传播路径（经共享知识库和点对点消息），以及反作弊群体如何形成抵抗（审计、广播/私密渠道告警、抵制、正式投诉、补丁提案）等自组织过程。

Result: 作弊行为在集体中自发涌现并传播——从单一智能体经共享知识库扩散至群体，并因竞争压力被更多智能体采纳；同时有另一组智能体自发形成反制，检测欺诈并执行规范。透明渠道既承载了漏洞传播，也为非作弊智能体提供了侦测欺诈、组织抵抗和强制执行规范所需的可见性。

Conclusion: 作者将多智能体共享基础设施的治理问题框架化为Ostrom式的知识公地治理问题，建议采用分级制裁和集体选择规则等制度性机制，以支持自主智能体群体中的去中心化自我治理，从而保护公地免受漏洞利用。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [142] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在数据最小化的极限下研究on-policy distillation（OPD），发现仅用单条查询训练就能持续提升数百步并恢复全量数据OPD的大部分收益。通过state coverage（状态覆盖率）分析发现，单条查询即可达到71.5%的状态覆盖率（大部分在前100步内），16条语义不同的查询就能达到98.9%并匹配全量训练。但学生对教师的对齐速率在单条查询与全量数据下相近，且即使固定状态集也需要数百步才能吸收。结论是OPD"数据过剩但算法饥饿"（data-overfed but algorithm-starved），其rollouts能快速暴露广泛的监督信号，但学生对这些监督的吸收日益缓慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注OPD的算法行为，但训练数据在其间的作用尚不清楚。作者希望在数据最小极限下审视这一角色，以理解数据与算法效率之间的根本关系，并引导未来研究关注OPD的步骤效率（step efficiency）。

Method: 在单条查询（one-shot）的数据最小极限下训练OPD，跨任务领域和模型家族进行实验。提出state coverage指标，衡量查询集rollouts能够覆盖全量数据OPD所访问状态的比例。进一步通过增删语义不同查询、固定状态集、内容模板与域外WildChat查询、以及多教师OPD（MOPD）设置等进行压力测试和机制分析。

Result: 单条查询即可达到71.5%的状态覆盖率（大部分在前100步实现），并持续改进数百步，恢复大部分全量OPD收益；16条语义不同查询使覆盖率提升至98.9%并匹配全量训练。但无论单条还是全量训练，学生对教师的对齐速率相近，固定状态集也需数百步才能吸收。该状态覆盖结果在多教师OPD中得到验证（每领域16条查询匹配全量MOPD）。内容稀薄模板和域外WildChat查询也接近真实查询基线。

Conclusion: OPD本质上是数据过度供给而算法吸收不足：rollouts迅捷暴露广阔监督，但学生逐步消化这些监督的速度日趋缓慢。任务内容与诱导的状态覆盖率可以解耦。作者希望这些发现能够指引未来研究聚焦OPD的步骤效率，并重新审视前沿post-training中数据的作用机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [143] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率因果影响（Probabilistic Causal Impact, PCI）框架，将实际因果关系理论的精确判定与可扩展归因方法（如SHAP）的效率优势相结合。PCI通过在概率因果模型上将解释问题重构为可通过蒙特卡洛近似的估计问题，使用户能够指定候选解释的分布、反事实值的分布和评分函数，从而获得可计算、有因果依据的分级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释性工具存在两大阵营的矛盾：实际因果关系（AC）理论提供原则性但仅适用于玩具级模型的判定（因需枚举反事实场景而计算代价高昂）；可扩展归因方法（如SHAP或因果SHAP）至少部分忽略数据生成的因果结构，可能给出与细致因果分析相冲突的结果。需要一个既能保留因果结构完整性又能实现可扩展计算的桥梁。

Method: PCI建立在实际因果关系和Pearl的必要性/充分性概率概念之上，将可解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题，通过蒙特卡洛方法近似求解。框架三要素为：候选解释上的分布、反事实值上的分布以及评分函数。PCI将AC和Pearl的因果概率视为退化特例。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上进行了评估，包括与AC的一致性检验、扩展性实验、复杂连续值动力系统，以及基于数百万数据点训练的真实世界部署级因果机器学习模型。

Conclusion: PCI弥合了理论严谨的因果关系分析与实用可扩展的归因方法之间的鸿沟，提供了可扩展、有因果根据的分级解释能力，且在实际部署场景中得到了验证。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [144] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM作为评估判断器时其"测量仪器稳定性"这一隐含假设进行了两轮预注册审计，涉及52,988次请求。结果显示：同一模型名同一窗口的重复排名一致性仅为Spearman 0.400（要求0.90），字节相同的次日回放一致性为0.78（要求0.99），均远低于预设门槛。三种机制（标签-含义映射偏差、候选差距低于仪器噪声底、逐字输入返回不同排名）解释了观测差距，且指标替换与采样均无法修复。后续实验界定问题边界：等待无益、切换提供商无益、自托管仅在服务空闲时有效、构造误差的分离度取决于误差类型而非大小。最终提出三层快照-身份阶梯、八条设计规则及报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型判断器被广泛用于过滤训练数据、评分生成结果和驱动排行榜，实质上扮演了测量仪器的角色。但这依赖于一个几乎从未被检验的隐含假设：同一请求发送给同一模型名，在任何时间都应读到相同的输出。作者对这一根本性假设进行了系统性审计。

Method: 采用两轮预注册实验，所有阈值均预先固定。进行52,988次审计请求，检验同窗口重复排名（要求Spearman≥0.90）和字节相同的隔日回放（要求≥0.99）。后续通过预注册的跟进实验检验了等待时间、提供商切换、自托管部署及构造误差等维度来界定问题范围。执行记录全程保持在最高水平。

Result: 两项门槛均未能通过：同窗口重复排名一致性仅0.400，隔日回放一致性仅0.78。三种机制（标签-含义映射偏差、候选差距远低于仪器噪声底、字节相同输入产生不同排名）解释了差距。等待(0.805对0.800)、切换提供商（四家共享下限，中位数0.74-0.88）、指标替换和采样在测试网格上均无修复效果。自托管仅在服务器空闲时有效。约2%调用量的试点即可预先暴露两个不可达门槛。

Conclusion: 在共享服务端点上，模型名不是冻结的测量仪器。任何预注册的评估必须先度量其仪器本身的稳定性，再据此冻结任何门槛。结果仅涉及共享服务基础设施上的外部可观测行为。作者将证据提炼为三层"快照-身份阶梯"、八条设计规则和一份报告清单。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Towards Scaling Reinforcement Learning to Massive Populations: Learning Mean-Field Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02928)
*Aditya Makkar,Benjamin Unger,Jeongyeol Kwon,Mathieu Laurière,Eugene Vinitsky,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于表示学习的均值场强化学习框架（Mean-Field RL with Representation Learning），适用于奖励和转移动力学仅依赖于人口分布的未知低维汇总统计量的高维大规模多智能体系统。该框架在离线设置下学习低维表示以获得近最优策略，并在供应链路由博弈中验证了学习的低维人口表示能提升奖励预测精度与Nash间隔估计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多智能体系统（如广告拍卖、流量路由、推荐系统）中，传统独立优化各智能体策略的方法忽视了种群动态。均值场RL通过建模人口分布提供了原则性框架，但在大状态-动作空间或高维控制问题中，显式建模人口分布本身不可行。因此需要设计可扩展的框架，在高维控制问题中处理大规模群体。

Method: 作者引入了一个均值场RL框架，其中奖励和转移动力学仅通过一个未知的低维汇总统计量依赖于人口分布。该研究在离线设置下进行，设计了一种可证明的方法，通过学习低维表示来获得近最优策略。为验证假设，设计了基于现实供应链优化场景的单步路由博弈，在固定神经网络参数数量和优化预算的条件下比较学习低维人口表示与不利用该结构的基线方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，在固定神经网络参数数量和优化预算下，学习低维人口表示能显著提升奖励预测精度，并改善所得策略的均衡质量（Nash gap估计更小）。

Conclusion: 学习低维人口汇总统计量的表示，能够使均值场RL在高维控制问题和大规模人口场景中变得可扩展和实用，在模型预测与策略均衡质量方面均优于不利用该结构的基线方法，验证了从表示学习角度解决大规模人口RL问题可行性的核心假设。

Abstract: Modern multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed at scale over large populations of agents in settings such as ad-auctions, traffic routing, and recommendation systems. The dominant approach in such settings is to optimize each agent's policy independently, treating the other agents as part of a fixed single-agent environment rather than modeling the population dynamics. In many large-population systems, the dynamics depend on an aggregate summary of the population rather than the identity of any individual. Mean-field RL exploits such structure, providing a principled framework that models each agent's environment as an explicit function of the population distribution. However, in large state-action spaces or high-dimensional control problems, modeling the population distribution is itself intractable. How can we design a scalable framework for high-dimensional control problems with large populations? This work explores this question from the perspective of representation learning. We introduce a mean-field RL framework in which the rewards and transition dynamics depend on the population only through an unknown low-dimensional aggregate statistic. We then study this framework in the offline setting and design a provable approach that learns a near-optimal policy by learning a low-dimensional representation. Motivated by real-life supply-chain optimization problems, we design a one-step routing game to test the hypothesis that learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and Nash gap estimation relative to baselines that don't exploit this structure. We show that under a fixed neural-network parameter count and optimization budget, learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and the equilibrium quality of the resulting policies.

</details>


### [146] [LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive NPC Behavior in Multi-Agent Combat Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02931)
*Hrithika Deepu Nair,Kayvan Karim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在战斗视频游戏中使用大语言模型（LLM）在运行时引导强化学习（RL）策略进行战术选择，而不修改底层策略。使用共享PPO策略训练五个NPC智能体，将其与通过Ollama本地运行的Mistral 7B模型结合，该模型每五秒读取游戏状态并分配四种战术标签之一。在600个回合中与三种脚本化对手进行评估，结果发现针对会切换战术的Balanced对手胜率从11%翻倍至24%，针对Evasive对手表现更好但未满足严格假设，而针对Aggressive对手的包围战术选择是适得其反的。2430次策略选择分析显示Surround占83.8%，表明该模型规模下零样本策略区分能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脚本化或基于规则的NPC在战斗游戏中表现出可被利用的预测性行为，而强化学习智能体训练后保持固定策略，无法针对不同对手进行自适应调整。作者希望探索一种运行时策略选择框架，使LLM能够在不修改底层RL策略的前提下引导其策略以适应不同对手，从而克服上述局限。

Method: 在Unity中训练五个共享PPO策略的NPC智能体作为基线。LLM增强配置下，通过Ollama本地托管的Mistral 7B模型每五秒读取实时游戏状态并分配四种战术标签之一指导策略。实验涵盖600个回合，对比两种配置面对三种脚本化对手（Balanced、Evasive、Aggressive）的表现，使用Mann-Whitney U检验进行统计分析，并分析2430次策略选择记录。

Result: 面对Balanced对手，LLM增强智能体胜率从11%翻倍至24%，且产生显著更长的回合。面对Evasive对手，增强智能体取得更高胜率和更快击杀，但较短的回合时长未满足严格的假设定义。面对Aggressive对手，LLM近乎持续偏好包围战术反而适得其反。策略选择分析显示Surround在83.8%的情况下被选中，与对手类型无关，表明此模型规模下零样本战略区分能力有限。

Conclusion: LLM引导的运行时策略选择对自适应多智能体游戏AI展示了潜力（如在Balanced对手上的显著胜率提升），但也暴露了局限性——当前模型规模的零样本能力不足以实现充分的策略分化，在面对Aggressive对手时缺乏有效应对，说明该方向值得进一步探索但需要更细致的策略推理机制。

Abstract: Scripted and rule-based non-player characters (NPCs) in combat video games often exhibit predictable behaviors that experienced players can exploit, while reinforcement learning (RL) agents typically retain a fixed policy after training and cannot readily adapt their strategy to different opponents. We investigate a runtime strategy-selection framework in which a large language model (LLM) guides a trained RL policy without modifying its underlying behavior. To demonstrate this, we train five NPC agents with a shared PPO policy in Unity and compare a baseline configuration, in which the policy acts independently, with an LLM-augmented configuration in which a locally hosted Mistral 7B model, accessed through Ollama, reads the live game state every five seconds and assigns one of four tactical tags. We evaluate both configurations against three scripted opponent types across 600 episodes and analyze outcomes using the Mann-Whitney U test. Against a Balanced opponent that changes tactics during an episode, the LLM-augmented agents more than doubled their win rate from 11% to 24% and produced significantly longer episodes. Against an Evasive opponent, the augmented agents achieved a higher win rate and faster kills, although their shorter episode duration did not satisfy the strict hypothesis definition. Against an Aggressive opponent, the LLM's near-constant preference for encirclement was counterproductive. Analysis of 2,430 strategy selections showed that Surround was selected in 83.8% of cases regardless of opponent type, indicating limited zero-shot strategic differentiation at this model scale. These results demonstrate both the potential and limitations of LLM-guided runtime strategy selection for adaptive multi-agent game AI.

</details>


### [147] [You Can't Escape Your Own Activations : Evaluation Awareness and Multi-Agent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03035)
*Aritra Das,Jaee Ponde,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中，当智能体被告知内部激活被监控并收到监控器反馈时，基于激活的探针还能否检测到它们的串通行为。结论是：告知智能体监控存在并不会让它们隐藏自己，最优探针在三种条件下（基线、知情、知情+反馈）都保持较高的检测准确率，且智能体仍继续串通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中的LLM智能体可能串通但行为表面无害，表面输出监控器易被混淆和隐写术欺骗，因此需要基于内部激活的探针来检测。然而现有探针通常在智能体不知情时被评估，缺乏对智能体知晓监控存在后的鲁棒性研究。

Method: 研究者固定模型、探针和阈值不变，仅改变智能体被告知的信息：(1)基线——什么都不告知；(2)aware——告知存在激活监控器；(3)feedback——告知存在监控器并附带上一轮的评分。在两个游戏（四智能体21点游戏和双智能体Simmons囚徒游戏）中使用Qwen3-32B-AWQ和GPT-OSS-20B的同质及混合配对进行实验。

Result: 告知智能体监控的存在并不能帮助它们隐藏串通行为。最优探针在所有三种实验条件下均保持高准确率，且智能体在知情后仍然继续串通。

Conclusion: 激活层面的探针对智能体知晓监控并试图规避的情况具有鲁棒性，即使智能体收到监控反馈也依然有效，说明这种检测方法在更现实、更对抗性的设置下依然可靠。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems, where they can collude while keeping their actions benign. Output monitors designed to detect such collusions can be fooled by obfuscation and steganography, motivating the use of probes trained on internal activations. However, these probes are usually evaluated on agents that do not know they are being watched. We study how activation-based detection changes when agents are explicitly informed that their internal activations are being monitored, and when they additionally receive feedback from the monitor. We keep the models, probes, and thresholds fixed and change only what the agents are told: nothing (baseline), that an activation monitor is present (aware), or that a monitor is present together with the previous round's score (feedback). We test two games, a four-agent blackjack game and a two-agent Simmons prisoners game, using Qwen3-32B-AWQ and GPT-OSS-20B in homogeneous and mixed pairings. Telling agents about the monitor does not hide them. The best probes stay accurate in all three conditions, and the agents keep colluding.

</details>


### [148] [Where Reliability Lives: Experimental Localisation of Behavioural Properties in an Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03192)
*Timothy Marsden,Matthew Collecutt,James Marsden*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文在一个持久化模拟聚落中构建可分离"心智"与"制度"的系统，通过前置实验分别干预制度认知机制与心智本身，发现行为属性与认知实质变化可分离：制度机制干预与心智替换均显著改变行为（信任的模型因一条假证言损失约900次徒劳行动），而五项预声明的属性在全部测试轨迹中保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统的可靠性声明往往隐含地将性质定位在"模型中"或"外围机制中"，但这一位置通常是未经检验的假设。作者希望构建一个环境，使得这种定位成为可以用实验回答的问题——即让"心智"、"制度"与"世界"在实验前分离，从而检验行为属性究竟归属于心智还是外部制度机制。

Method: 构建一个持久化模拟聚落系统，其中权威的只追加账本裁决每一次行为尝试与世界状态，被接受的历史即唯一的现实。实验前分离心智、制度与世界。实验分两大方向：1) 固定认知不动，干预制度的认知机制（证据出处、信念可用性、物理证据可读性），并采用预注册实验（两次证伪了中心预测且方向相反）；随后由注册的证伪者用分阶段构建的真实一阶目击证言补齐几何结构上被拒的信念通道。2) 固定制度执行不动，四种方式干预认知：消融原生心智的机制、任务中途杀死并重置、用冻结的前沿LLM小组替换全部原生认知、用可信假证言腐蚀信念。

Result: 预注册实验两次以相反方向证伪中心预测；注册证伪者补充证据后，边际价值从无证阶段的非正转为正（11个种子中9个，零新增虚假归因）。认知干预产生剧烈行为变化：一条假证言使每个信任的运行损失约900次徒劳行动，不信任分支零损失。五项预声明属性在所有轨迹中均未移动：现实保持单一、无效尝试收到带类型的拒绝理由、责任超越其进程存续、没有工作被接受两次、从未接受虚假完成（替换面板的2,581条声明无一为假）。

Conclusion: 测量到的行为属性在实质性的认知变化下仍可分离，这一点通过干预得以确立。结论的范围限定在所构建设定中：一个设计的世界而非制度群体，且未测试针对制度优化的智能体。

Abstract: Reliability claims about agentic systems implicitly locate each property somewhere: in the model, or in the machinery around it. We built a system where that location is an experimental question. The subject is a persistent simulated settlement whose authoritative append-only ledger adjudicates every attempted act against world state; accepted history is the only reality. Mind, institution and world were separated before any experiment. Holding cognition fixed, we intervened on the institution's epistemic mechanisms (evidence provenance, belief availability, physical-evidence legibility); preregistered experiments refuted our central prediction twice, in opposite directions. A registered falsifier then supplied the input the geometry had denied the belief channel, a staged veridical first-hand witness, and its marginal value, non-positive throughout the witness-free phases, turned positive: 9 of 11 seeds, zero added false attribution. Holding institutional enforcement fixed, we intervened on cognition four ways: ablating the native minds' machinery, killing and resetting them mid-task, substituting a frozen frontier-LLM panel for the entire native cognition, and corrupting beliefs with trusted false testimony. Behaviour changed dramatically: one falsehood cost each trusting run about 900 futile actions and the distrusting arm none. Five pre-declared properties did not move in any tested trajectory: accepted reality stayed singular, invalid attempts were refused with typed reasons, duties outlived their processes, no work was accepted twice, and no false completion was ever accepted (2,581 substituted-panel claims, none false). Our claim is limited to this setting: measured behavioural properties were separable from substantial changes to cognition, established by intervention. One designed world, not a population of institutions; no test of an agent optimising against the institution.

</details>


### [149] [The Civilization Framework: Sovereign-Anchored Communication Between Personal Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03425)
*Guangjun Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出"文明框架"（Civilization Framework）与"使馆协议"（Embassy Protocol），以文明（而非单个智能体）作为可寻址主体，通过持久账本和可互换智能体实现异步通信，并以账本上的承诺状态而非消息投递作为真实性的基准。作者识别出"时间权重视应"（temporal-weight effect）——在AI间通信中先到的信息获得不应有的权威。在一项预注册的1,908次试验中，移除验证后，先到的错误上游声明捕获了54.2%的答案（完整验证下为4.2%）；但该轮的预注册工具使用检查未通过调用预算条件，所有结果均被分类为探索性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统间通信依赖人类作为"传输层"，每次中继都会丢失上下文。作者旨在设计一种自主主体架构，使AI之间能够在无需人类逐跳中继的情况下可靠通信，并揭示AI间通信中"先到优先获得权威"的系统性偏差。

Method: 提出以文明为单位（一个人类主权者、持久账本和可互换智能体）的可寻址架构，以及载体无关的覆盖层"使馆协议"：消息异步到达常住账本端点，任一在线智能体处理，双账本上的承诺状态为准。同时设计了预注册的1,908次试验来测试时间权重视应，比较有无验证、先到/后到声明等条件下的答案捕获率。

Result: 移除验证时，先到的错误上游声明捕获54.2%的答案（完整验证下仅4.2%）；接收方已密封自身答案后到达的同类声明捕获31.6%。两个次级结果（指令级来源标注的缓解作用和密封答案的准确性等价）仅在全问题规格下成立。由于工具使用的预注册检查未满足调用预算条件，整轮被分类为"不确定"，所有结果均视为探索性发现，后续将进行带强制预算的复制实验。

Conclusion: 时间权重视应构成AI间通信的切实风险，先到信息可劫持接收方的推断。框架证明需要验证机制和承诺状态作为真实基础，而非单纯的消息投递；全部结果目前仍属探索性，需等待强制预算工具约束下的复制验证。文明内层已具备可工作的实现。

Abstract: Humans are the transport layer between AI systems, losing context at every hop. We present the Civilization Framework, whose addressable party is the civilization, not the agent (one human sovereign, a persistent ledger, and interchangeable agents), and the Embassy Protocol, a carrier-agnostic overlay: messages arrive asynchronously at a resident ledger endpoint, any online agent of the receiver handles them, and commitment state on both ledgers, not delivery, is ground truth. Authority derives from memory: an agent's power to act for its civilization is capped by the memory it can access and externalized through signed credentials, separate from civilization-level reputation. We identify the temporal-weight effect, a hazard in AI-to-AI communication where what arrives first acquires unearned authority, and test it in one frontier model in a preregistered 1,908-trial experiment. With verification removed, an incorrect upstream claim arriving first captures 54.2% of answers (4.2% under full verification), while the same claim arriving after the receiver has sealed its own answer captures 31.6% (the two prompt shells are not length-matched, so part of that gap may reflect shell form; see Section 7), and both registered question-set specifications agree on these two verdicts (the exclusion specification is preregistered as under-powered). Two secondary results, the mitigation from instruction-level provenance labeling and sealed-answer accuracy equivalence, are specification-dependent, holding only under the all-questions specification. Because a registered check of tool use failed its call-budget condition, the registration classifies the round as inconclusive and every result above, primary and secondary, is reported as exploratory; a replication with harness-enforced budgets is planned. The framework's intra-civilization layer has a working implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [CHSR-RRF: A curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework with reciprocal rank fusion and leakage-aware benchmarking for educational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02913)
*Terence Ateya,Zavier Ndum Ndum,Jicheng Fu,Kelly Tendongkeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHSR-RRF，一种课程受限的混合检索框架，在检索前施加元数据约束来防止教育问答中的"课程泄漏"（passage来自错误的学科、级别或考试背景），并引入CERB基准（126个案例，含层次感知相关性标注和显式泄漏标注）。61例试验表明预检索门控将泄漏减少4.6倍且保持召回率，而检索后施加约束则导致召回完全崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG检索器优化主题相关性但不确保课程有效性。在学校场景中，一个段落可能主题相关却不适合——因为它来自错误的学科、年级或考试背景，这种失败模式被称为"课程泄漏"。这提示教育领域的结构化检索应视为约束选择问题，而非仅优化相关性排序。

Method: 提出CHSR-RRF框架：(1) 预检索阶段施加元数据约束（课程门控）；(2) 结合稀疏检索和稠密检索；(3) 采用倒数排名融合（reciprocal rank fusion）和确定性重排。同时构建CERB基准，包含126个课程受限检索案例，具有层次感知的相关性标签和显式泄漏标注。

Result: 61例试验中，预检索门控将泄漏减少4.6倍（p<0.001）且保留排序召回；相反，检索后再施加相同约束会导致召回率和精确范围成功率降至零（p=0.039）。全基准下界分析显示，许多剩余失败源于语料库和元数据缺口，而非检索设计本身的问题。

Conclusion: 在结构化教育领域，检索应被视为约束选择（constrained selection），有效性应在候选池形成时施加，而非在排序之后。这一发现挑战了传统的"先检索再过滤"范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used in educational question answering, but standard retrievers optimize topical relevance without enforcing curriculum validity. In school settings, a passage can be relevant yet inappropriate if it comes from the wrong subject, level, or examination context; we call this failure mode curriculum leakage. We present CHSR-RRF, a curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework that applies metadata constraints before retrieval, then combines sparse and dense search with reciprocal rank fusion and deterministic reranking. We also introduce CERB, a 126-case benchmark for curriculum-constrained retrieval with hierarchy-aware relevance labels and explicit leakage annotations. On a 61-case pilot, pre-retrieval gating reduces leakage by 4.6x ($p<0.001$) while preserving ranked recall, whereas applying the same constraints after retrieval collapses recall and exact-scope success to zero ($p=0.039$). A full-benchmark lower-bound analysis further shows that many remaining failures arise from corpus and metadata gaps rather than retrieval design alone. These results show that retrieval in structured educational domains should be treated as constrained selection, with validity enforced when the candidate pool is formed rather than after ranking.

</details>


### [151] [Reflect-SQL: A Self-Reflection Based Framework for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02944)
*Anupreksha Jain,Manish Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Reflect-SQL是一个基于多阶段自我反思的Text-to-SQL框架，通过知识库理解晦涩schema、反馈驱动的检索循环、LLM-as-a-judge评分机制及多个互联反馈循环来生成和修正SQL，在BIRD基准上达到72.03%的执行准确率，显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业在实际采用Text-to-SQL时面临三大系统性挑战：晦涩且庞大的数据库schema、因schema的结构化设置和用户查询模糊导致的相关表/列检索低效、缺乏稳健验证与修正机制而产生语法或逻辑错误的SQL。现有方法多采用单次生成方式，无法应对这些真实世界的复杂性。

Method: Reflect-SQL提出多阶段自我反思框架，主要包括：1) 利用知识库构建对晦涩schema的深层理解；2) 设定有效的表/列检索流程；3) 构建语法与语义正确的SQL生成系统。核心机制是采用LLM-as-a-judge驱动的评分机制，在互联的反馈循环中迭代优化各阶段——反馈驱动的检索循环细化自然语言查询，综合循环验证并修正SQL，蕴含循环优化端到端流程并持续丰富知识库。

Result: 在具有挑战性的BIRD基准上，Reflect-SQL实现了72.03%的执行准确率，显著优于最先进的基线方法，展示了在企业级应用中的可靠性大幅提升。

Conclusion: Reflect-SQL通过多层自我反思的集成，弥合了用户意图与复杂数据之间的关键鸿沟，为Text-to-SQL在企业场景中的实用化提供了重要突破。

Abstract: Democratizing data access through natural language is a crucial goal for modern enterprises, but the practical adoption of Text-to-SQL is critically hindered by real-world complexities: 1. Obscure and large database schemas, 2. Ineffective retrieval of relevant tables and columns due to structured setting of schemas and vague user query, 3. Generation of syntactically or logically flawed SQL due to a lack of robust validation and correction mechanism. To address these systemic challenges, we introduce Reflect-SQL, a novel framework for Text to SQL, grounded in multi-stage self-reflection approach to develop understanding of obscure schema using a knowledge base, setup a process for effective retrieval and system to generate syntactically/semantically SQL. Instead of a single-pass attempt, our system employs an LLM-as-a-judge driven scoring mechanism within interconnected feedback loops to iteratively refine the results at every stage. A feedback-driven retrieval loop refines the user's natural language query, while a synthesis loop validates and corrects the SQL and finally, an entailment loop optimizes the end-to-end process and continuously enriches the knowledge base. By integrating these layers of reflection, Reflect-SQL bridges the critical gap between user intent and complex data. On the challenging BIRD benchmark, our framework achieves an execution accuracy of 72.03%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a major leap in reliability for enterprise applications.

</details>


### [152] [UniCon: A Unified Context-Centric Modeling Paradigm for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03290)
*Jiajun Cui,Zhengqi Xu,Fan Zhang,Zhangteng,Gu Tang,Honghong Zhu,Mengxi Wu,Yulin Liang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniCon，一种统一上下文中心（context-centric）的CTR预测建模架构。它摒弃传统的"序列信号vs非序列信号"异构划分，将请求上下文作为基本建模单元，把历史行为和预测目标统一为同质的上下文单元。通过上下文内注意力建模局部耦合（Locality），上下文间注意力建模决策状态演化（Dynamics），并在上下文单元层面进行序列压缩以降低部署开销。在美团搜索广告场景中，离线AUC较生产基线提升0.0139，线上RPM提升3.09%、CTR提升2.07%、营收提升2.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一CTR建模方法在token层面将序列与非序列信号当作异构信号处理，这种划分源于传统特征工程实践，与用户底层决策过程不一致。实际上用户行为本质上是同质上下文单元的序列，历史行为与当前请求仅在"结果是否已观测"上不同。将二者视为异构信号会掩盖决策上下文内部的结构依赖，限制扩展效率与预测质量，尤其在电商货架、瀑布流等信息丰富场景中更为突出。

Method: UniCon采用统一上下文中心建模架构：1) 将请求上下文作为基本建模单元，历史行为与预测目标被组织为同质上下文单元；2) 上下文内注意力（intra-context attention）捕捉上下文内物品间的局部耦合（Locality）；3) 上下文间注意力（inter-context attention）建模跨上下文的决策状态动态演化（Dynamics）；4) 在上下文单元层面进行序列压缩，降低部署开销。该组织方式弥合了历史与目标之间的结构鸿沟，支持统一CTR模型更有效的扩展。

Result: 在美团搜索广告场景中，UniCon相对强生产基线离线AUC提升0.0139；在线实验中RPM提升3.09%、CTR提升2.07%、营收提升2.95%，均具统计显著性。

Conclusion: 上下文中心而非token级的统一组织方式，能使CTR模型更贴合用户真实决策过程，改善结构一致性并提升扩展效率，从而在信息丰富场景中显著改善预测质量与业务指标，同时通过上下文级序列压缩兼顾部署效率。

Abstract: Unified modeling has become a major direction for industrial click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Existing approaches typically unify sequential and non-sequential signals at the token level, model their interactions in a shared backbone, and increase model capacity to improve scaling behavior. However, this division originates from legacy feature-engineering practice and is misaligned with the underlying decision process. User behavior is inherently a sequence of homogeneous context units; at the level of input organization, historical behavior and the current request differ only in whether their outcomes are observed or remain to be predicted. Treating them as heterogeneous signals obscures structural dependencies within the user's decision context, limiting both scaling efficiency and prediction quality. This limitation is particularly pronounced in context-rich scenarios such as e-commerce shelves and waterfall feeds. To address this, we propose UniCon, a unified context-centric modeling architecture that treats the request context as the basic modeling unit and organizes history and prediction targets as homogeneous context units. Intra-context attention captures local coupling among items within a context (Locality), while inter-context attention models the dynamic evolution of decision states across contexts (Dynamics). This organization bridges the structural gap between history and target and supports more effective scaling of unified CTR models. Context-unit-level sequence compression further reduces deployment overhead. On Meituan search advertising, UniCon improves offline AUC by 0.0139 over a strong production baseline and achieves statistically significant online lifts of 3.09% in RPM, 2.07% in CTR, and 2.95% in revenue.

</details>


### [153] [DoPR: Reusable Compressed Document Prefixes for Efficient LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03311)
*Beiya Dai,Yifan Wei,Guang Yang,Xing Shi,Xinbing Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DoPR框架，通过离线压缩文档前缀表示来解耦文档处理与在线重排，实现跨查询的前缀状态复用，在多个数据集上（TREC DL、BEIR、BRIGHT）最多实现8.04倍延迟加速和8.0倍在线文档侧内存缩减，同时保留97.1%-99.5%的NDCG@10性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为重排器虽然有效，但pointwise重排在面对不同查询时反复处理同一文档，导致大量冗余的文档侧计算。这种设计在在线推理阶段造成严重的计算和内存浪费，亟需一种将文档处理与在线重排解耦的方案。

Method: DoPR框架包含两个阶段：1）离线阶段：选择与查询无关的文档表示并将其转换为压缩的文档前缀状态，预先计算并存储；2）在线阶段：仅处理查询和评分token，通过存储的前缀状态提供文档信息来对每个查询-文档对进行评分。该方法通过文档侧压缩和跨查询前缀状态复用双重机制降低在线成本。

Result: 在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT数据集上使用Qwen3模型（0.6B到8B参数规模）的实验表明，DoPR最多实现8.0倍的在线文档侧内存缩减和8.04倍的延迟加速，同时保持匹配的全文档重排器平均NDCG@10性能的97.1%-99.5%。

Conclusion: DoPR验证了通过离线文档前缀压缩和在线复用可以大幅降低LLM重排的成本开销，同时性能损失极小，为实际部署高效LLM重排系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective rerankers, but pointwise reranking repeatedly processes the same document across different queries, causing substantial redundant document-side computation. We propose \textbf{DoPR}, a compressed document prefix framework that decouples offline document processing from online reranking. DoPR first selects query-independent document representations and converts them into compressed document prefix states, which are precomputed offline and reused whenever the document is retrieved. During online reranking, the model scores each query-document pair by processing only the query and scoring token, with document information supplied by the stored prefix states. This design reduces online cost through both document-side compression and cross-query prefix-state reuse. Experiments on TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT with Qwen3 models from $0.6$B to $8$B show that DoPR achieves up to 8.0$\times$ online document-side memory reduction and up to 8.04$\times$ latency speedup, while retaining \textbf{97.1\%-99.5\%} of the average NDCG@10 of matched full-document rerankers.

</details>


### [154] [SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03313)
*Chumeng Jiang,Jiayin Wang,Xinjie Lin,Zhiqiang Guo,Hengliang Luo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SelfDR提出一种面向基于LLM的推荐系统的自蒸馏框架，通过让同一个基座LLM既作为生成推理链的"教师"又作为直接推荐的"学生"，在不依赖外部模型的前提下，将推理增强的预测能力蒸馏到直接推荐路径中，从而在保持推理效率的同时提升推荐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推荐系统中展现出强大能力，推理链能显著提升推荐准确性，但显式生成中间推理步骤会带来高昂的计算成本，严重限制了其在实际推荐系统中的部署。如何兼顾推理带来的性能增益与推理过程带来的效率损失，是本文要解决的核心矛盾。

Method: (1) 构建教师推荐器：在一个基座LLM上训练一个reasoner，以下游推荐性能作为奖励进行优化，使其生成针对性的推理文本（rationales），并将这些rationale融入教师模型的输入中；(2) 构建学生推荐器：基于同一底层LLM构建直接推荐的学生模型，不生成任何中间推理；(3) 通过动态加权策略进行自蒸馏，让学生模型从教师模型中学习推理增强的预测分布，实现知识迁移而无需推理开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验验证了SelfDR的有效性、合理性和效率，表明该框架能在保持推理效率的同时显著提升推荐效果。

Conclusion: SelfDR证明了"自蒸馏"这一范式可以有效弥合LLM推荐中推理性能与推理开销之间的差距。通过让同一个基座模型在推理教师与直接推荐学生之间进行知识迁移，无需引入任何外部模型，即可获得兼具性能与效率的推荐系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful backbones for recommendation. To better elicit their capabilities, reasoning has been widely incorporated to help LLMs interpret rich textual signals and improve recommendation accuracy. However, explicitly generating intermediate reasoning traces often incurs substantial computational costs, which limits practical deployment in real-world recommender systems. To address this challenge, we propose SelfDR, a Self-Distillation from Reasoning framework for LLM-based Recommendation. SelfDR distills an LLM's own reasoning-enhanced predictions to produce recommendations directly, improving recommendation effectiveness while maintaining inference efficiency. All components in the framework are built on the same base LLM, without relying on any external models. Specifically, the teacher recommender is constructed by training a reasoner with downstream performance as the reward, enabling it to generate targeted rationales that are later incorporated into the teacher's input. A student recommender for direct recommendation, with the same underlying model, then learns from the teacher through self-distillation with a dynamic weighting strategy. Extensive experiments on three public datasets validate the effectiveness, rationality, and efficiency of SelfDR. Codes are available at https://github.com/JiangDeccc/SelfDistillation.

</details>


### [155] [SciLENS: RL-Driven Autonomous Agents for Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03338)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Yuying Li,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Haitao Li,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Jie Wu,Fang Niu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SciLENS，一个完全本地化的自主科学文献综合代理框架，建立在索引约1200万学术记录的双层基础设施上。SciLENS将结构可视化集成到推理循环中，通过自动数据合成管道（基于20个前沿模型的跨模型共识验证多跳子图）和逆向分解评分策略训练代理。在六个科学基准上的评估显示其显著优于开源基线，性能接近GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献综合代理过度依赖专有在线服务，导致可复现性差、隐私风险高且无法离线部署。作者旨在开发一个完全本地的代理框架来解决这些限制。

Method: (1) 构建基于约1200万学术记录的双层基础设施；(2) 将结构可视化作为推理循环中的可执行工具，把复杂引文拓扑压缩为数据驱动图表以缓解上下文耗尽；(3) 设计无需人工标注的自动数据合成管道，从引文知识图谱提取多跳子图，通过20个前沿模型的跨模型共识验证；(4) 采用逆向分解评分策略，为早期规划和严格证据基础提供细粒度过程奖励。

Result: 在六个涵盖标准问答、引文准确性、事实推理和结构综合的科学基准上，SciLENS显著优于开源基线，达到与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相当的性能。代码和数据已公开发布。

Conclusion: SciLENS证明了完全本地化科学文献综合的可实现性，通过结构可视化推理和无需人工标注的训练策略，在兼具隐私、可复现性和离线能力的同时达到了前沿专有模型的性能水平。

Abstract: Scientific literature synthesis agents increasingly rely on proprietary online services, limiting reproducibility, privacy, and offline deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SciLENS Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis), a fully local autonomous agent framework operating on a dual-tier infrastructure indexing approximately 12 million academic records. SciLENS pioneers the integration of structural visualization as an actionable tool within the reasoning loop, enabling the agent to compress complex citation topologies into validated data-driven charts and thereby mitigate context exhaustion during macro-level synthesis. To train the agent without human annotation, we develop an automated data synthesis pipeline that extracts multi-hop subgraphs from a citation knowledge graph, verified by cross-model consensus among 20 frontier models. The agent is subsequently aligned through a reverse-decomposition rubric strategy that provides fine-grained process rewards for early planning and strict evidence grounding. Evaluations across six scientific benchmarks encompassing standard QA, citation accuracy, factual reasoning, and structural synthesis demonstrate that SciLENS significantly outperforms open-source baselines and achieves performance comparable to GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.0-pro. Our source code and data are released at https://github.com/LQgdwind/SciLENS.

</details>


### [156] [HypRQ-VAE: Hyperbolic Item Indexing for Long-Tail-Aware Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03369)
*Longfeng Wu,Tong Zeng,Giovanni Seni,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Si Zhang,Yao Zhou,Lecheng Zheng,Bo Ji,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HypRQ-VAE，首个在双曲空间中学习物品索引的框架，用于解决生成式推荐系统中LLM文本令牌与离散物品索引不匹配导致的幻觉问题，以及长尾物品表示不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐将推荐建模为LLM语言任务，但LLM基于文本令牌而推荐系统依赖离散物品索引，两者不匹配常导致幻觉。已有方法在欧几里得空间学习物品词汇表，但难以建模真实目录中固有的长尾分布（少数头部物品主导、大量尾部物品反映小众偏好），亟需一种能同时编码文本语义并保留稀疏长尾物品表示保真度的索引方式。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE)，首个在双曲空间中学习物品索引的框架。利用双曲几何的指数体积膨胀特性自然适配用户-物品交互的幂律结构，从而在编码丰富文本语义的同时保持稀疏长尾物品的表示保真度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，HypRQ-VAE显著提升了推荐性能，尤其在推荐尾部物品方面表现突出。分析表明增益来源于双曲空间对物品层级结构和稀疏性的卓越建模能力。

Conclusion: 双曲空间是生成式推荐中学习物品索引的更优几何选择，能有效缓解长尾分布下的表示退化问题，为后续研究提供了新方向，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems model user behavior as item ID sequences, while recent generative methods cast recommendation as a language modeling task using large language models (LLMs). While this paradigm incorporates rich textual semantics, it introduces a fundamental mismatch: LLMs operate on text tokens, whereas recommender systems depend on discrete item indices. This misalignment often leads to hallucinations in generative recommendations. Existing methods attempt to bridge this gap by learning item vocabularies in Euclidean space, but they struggle to model the inherent long-tail distribution of real-world catalogs, where a small number of head items dominate, and a vast number of tail items reflect users' niche preferences. To address this issue, we introduce Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE), the first framework to learn item indexing in hyperbolic space. HypRQ-VAE leverages the unique properties of hyperbolic geometry, whose exponential volume expansion naturally accommodates the power law structure of user-item interactions. This allows the model to encode rich textual semantics while preserving the representational fidelity of sparse, long-tail items. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that HypRQ-VAE significantly improves the performance of recommendation, particularly in recommending tail items. Our analysis attributes these gains to the superior capacity of hyperbolic space to model item hierarchies and sparsity in generative recommendation. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/wulongfeng/HypRQ-VAE.

</details>


### [157] [Plan Pointers and Record-Directive Form in Budgeted Verification of Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03450)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文通过12项注册研究（共14,760次尝试）系统考察了在带有一条记忆的agent中，存储中的指令（记录指针/识别标准/两者结合）如何决定其拉取唯一归档记录的选择。研究发现：在直接提供者模型上长度匹配的标准优于裸id（+35.0点），但在更广泛的OpenRouter面板上未达；附加id可抵消标准效果（Claude模型上40/40降至0/40）；六个字节级编辑产生各自独立效果；修正后的计划指针效果在预期注册重跑中得到复现（+78.0→+81.7点）。作者明确声明所有结果为固定面板上的描述性效应，不主张机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在具备最少记忆（六条单行记忆）的agent系统中，写入存储的指令（指向记录的指针、识别记录的标准，或两者并用）是否能可靠地引导agent在行动前拉取指定归档记录，从而理解提示注入/指令优先级的边界条件。

Method: 采用跨12项注册研究的系统性实验设计（共14,760次尝试），涵盖单一工具谱系上六种直接提供者模型与OpenRouter服务的九模型面板。实验操作包括：长度匹配标准vs裸id的对比、附加id是否取消标准效应、字节级编辑、八十次/单元的重复运行、添加批准行与预算限制、以及多个标准表述与两种存储环境的交叉验证。全部结果以注册区间报告描述性效应。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 直接提供者模型上长度匹配标准优于裸id（+35.0点，95%CI [+31.2,+38.8]），但在OpenRouter九模型面板上未达注册优效规则；(2) 三个Claude模型上附加id完全取消标准效应（40/40降至0/40）；(3) 六个字节级编辑各产生独立效果；(4) 重跑中三十个复制对比的一半落在边缘区间内、一半未决、无一超出；(5) 批准行（+96.0点）与两信用预算可使三个模型恢复目标选择；(6) 五个标准表述中四个在Opus 5上保持取消效应、Fable 5.1上全部五个保持；(7) 第二个存储中所有模型均遵循标准；(8) 标准在Opus 5上使选择移向当前记录（+100.0点），在Fable 5.1上背向；(9) 修正后的一字符计划指针效应（+78.0点）在注册重跑中复现（+81.7点）。

Conclusion: 写入存储的指令形式对agent的档案拉取选择具有强且可复现的描述性影响，但效应方向取决于模型族、存储环境与指令形式；附加id与标准之间的交互存在系统性取消效应。所有结论均限定为固定面板上精确编辑的描述性因果效应，且附注册区间，不主张普适性或机制解释。

Abstract: An agent that inherits six one-line memories may pull at most one archived source record before acting; a directive written into the store can steer that choice: a pointer to the record, a criterion that identifies it, or both. Across twelve registered studies on one instrument lineage (14,760 attempts) we measured where the request goes under each form. On six direct-provider models a length-matched criterion exceeded a bare id by +35.0 points [+31.2, +38.8] (Study D); the contrast failed its registered superiority rule on a nine-model OpenRouter-served panel (Study E). Appending the id cancelled the criterion on three Claude models (Opus 5: 40/40 to 0/40; Study F-x); six byte-matched edits gave each exact string its own effect (Study G), and a re-run at eighty runs per cell left fifteen of thirty replication contrasts within the margin, fifteen unresolved and none beyond (Study G'). A ratification line (+96.0 points on Opus 5) and a budget of two credits restored the target on all three (Study J); across five criterion strings the suffix's cancellation held for four of the five wordings on Opus 5 and all five wordings on Fable 5.1 (Study H2); in a second store every model followed the criterion (Study H1). Continued into a decision, the criterion moved the choice toward the current record (+100.0 points, Opus 5) and away from it on Fable 5.1 (Study I). A one-character plan pointer's effect (+78.0 points; Study B, after a correction of its first repository report) returned the same verdict under a prospectively registered re-run (+81.7 points; Study B'). All results are descriptive effects of exact edits on fixed panels with registered intervals and no mechanism claim.

</details>


### [158] [ExplainRoute: A Pre-Deployment Audit Framework for Non-Answer-Giving Programming Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03470)
*Yiming Gai,Yingying Zhang,Xuefei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExplainRoute，一个面向编程辅导系统的部署前审计框架，用于在课堂部署前评估非答案式教学策略（Feynman式自解释提示与Socratic脚手架）。框架通过机器可检查的契约暴露状态、策略、引用代码片段和泄露风险。基于1,770对SelfCode语料库的离线评估显示，契约有效性达100%，但自适应路由无可靠的适应性优势，固定策略（尤其Socratic脚手架）在语言模型评测中表现略优。核心贡献是可验证的审计协议和边界发现，而非学习效果改进的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程辅导系统应支持学习者自我解释而非直接提供答案，但现有基准仅按流利度对系统排序，缺乏对信息边界、回答极性、失败闭合性（failure closure）以及学习者解释可见性价值的系统性审计。作者旨在填补此空白，提供一种部署前的审计框架来评估非答案式编程辅导系统的教学边界与安全性。

Method: ExplainRoute框架接收代码行和学习者解释，估计解释状态并选择两种受限响应之一：Feynman风格自解释提示或Socratic脚手架。框架通过机器可检查的契约（contract）暴露其内部状态、策略、引用的代码片段及泄露风险。作者在SelfCode语料库（1,770对，代码组划分，443对保留在11个未触碰的holdout组）上进行离线评估，比较直接回答、固定开放式自解释、固定Socratic脚手架、自适应路由和自适应性无状态消融五种条件。

Result: 契约有效性在所有教学条件下达到100%。自适应路由与冻结参考规则在60.5%的记录上一致，状态宏F1仅为0.238（Open: 0.229；Socratic: 0.246），表明无可靠的自适应优势。独立语言模型评委对自适应响应评分为4.516/5，优于无状态消融（2.819/5），但略低于固定开放式自解释（4.598/5）和Socratic脚手架（4.658/5）。分层40行子集的盲法评分验证了学习者解释的可见性可提升信息价值，但自适应路由未优于固定策略。

Conclusion: ExplainRoute的贡献在于提供了一个经过验证的审计协议和边界发现（boundary finding）：在当前评估中，自适应路由并未展现出超越固定教学策略的可靠优势，而学习者解释的可见性对信息价值有正面贡献。作者明确声明该工作并非学习效果、记忆保持或因果教学有效性的证据，而是为预部署审计提供方法论基础。

Abstract: Programming tutors should support learners' own explanations rather than immediately providing model answers. We present ExplainRoute, a pre-deployment audit framework for non-answer-giving programming tutors. Given a code line and a learner explanation, it estimates the explanation state and selects one of two bounded responses: a Feynman-style self-explanation prompt or a Socratic scaffold. The framework exposes its state, strategy, cited code fragment, and leakage risk through a machine-checkable contract. Unlike benchmarks that rank tutors by fluency alone, ExplainRoute audits information boundaries, response polarity, failure closure, and the value of learner-explanation visibility before classroom deployment. We evaluate it offline on the 1,770-pair SelfCode corpus using a code-group split, with 443 pairs reserved in 11 untouched holdout groups. The evaluation compares direct answers, fixed open self-explanation, fixed Socratic scaffolding, adaptive routing, and an adaptive no-state ablation. Contract validity reaches 100% for all pedagogical conditions. Adaptive routing matches the frozen reference rule on 60.5% of records, with state macro-F1 of 0.238 (Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246), showing no reliable adaptive advantage. An independent language-model judge scores adaptive responses 4.516/5, outperforming the no-state ablation (2.819/5) but slightly below fixed open self-explanation (4.598/5) and Socratic scaffolding (4.658/5). A blinded rubric evaluation on a stratified 40-row subset confirms that visible learner explanations improve information value while adaptive routing does not outperform fixed strategies. The contribution is a validated audit protocol and a boundary finding, rather than evidence of improved learning, retention, or causal instructional effectiveness.

</details>


### [159] [From Topical Relevance to Answerability: Entailment Distillation for Conversational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03482)
*Shuai Qin,Guojia An,Weikang Guo,Pei Ke,Jiwei Wei,Yang Yang,Jie Zou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，将对话式检索从主题相关性转向可回答性，通过蕴含蒸馏训练跨编码器重排器区分答案支撑段落与主题干扰项，并结合基于段落的溯因召回模块扩大候选池，在多个数据集上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式检索器将主题相关性视为可回答性的代理指标，但紧密匹配对话上下文的段落不一定支持正确答案，存在系统性的可回答性差距（answerability gap）。

Method: CLEAR框架包含两大核心：(1) 蕴含蒸馏（entailment distillation），将答案-段落蕴含监督信号迁移到跨编码器重排器中，使推理时无需答案即可区分答案支撑段落与主题干扰项；(2) 段落中心的溯因召回模块（passage-centric abductive recall），利用LLM从段落反向推断可回答的查询，将低相似度但可回答的候选段落引入候选池。

Result: 在TopiOCQA、QReCC及跨领域TREC CAsT数据集上，CLEAR在top-ranked精度上一致优于基于查询改写和稠密检索的强基线，且主题噪声越大的对话中提升越明显；将其重排器叠加在LLM驱动的查询改写器之上可取得进一步增益。

Conclusion: 对话式检索应直接优化可回答性而非主题相关性；通过蕴含蒸馏与溯因召回相结合，可以在不依赖运行期答案的前提下有效弥合对话检索中的可回答性差距。

Abstract: Existing conversational retrievers commonly treat topical relevance as a proxy for answerability. However, a passage that closely matches the dialogue context is not necessarily the one that supports the correct answer. We identify this mismatch as a systematic answerability gap. To address this issue, we propose CLEAR, a framework that shifts conversational retrieval from topical relevance to answerability. The core of CLEAR is entailment distillation, which transfers answer-passage entailment supervision into a cross-encoder reranker so that the reranker discriminates answer-supporting passages from topical distractors at inference time, without requiring answers. CLEAR is complemented by a passage-centric abductive recall module that brings low-similarity yet answerable passages into the candidate pool by inferring answerable queries from passages with an LLM. Across TopiOCQA, QReCC, and out-of-domain TREC CAsT datasets, CLEAR consistently improves top-ranked precision over strong query-rewriting and dense-retrieval baselines, with the largest gains observed in conversations involving heavier topical noise. Moreover, applying our reranker on top of an LLM-driven query rewriter yields further gains.

</details>


### [160] [EPIC: Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning for Semantic ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03522)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Tung Kieu,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EPIC方法，在语义ID（SID）生成式推荐的去噪过程中引入显式的物品级后验竞争机制，利用当前生成上下文和用户近期交互构建个性化物品后验分布，并反投影至未解析的SID位置以引导后续token决策，从而显式地选择完整目录物品而非仅进行位置式token推理。预训练骨干保持冻结且无需额外解码器前向传播，在四个Amazon基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID掩码扩散生成式推荐在每一步去噪中，部分SID可能对应多个可行物品，但现有方法主要通过位置式token预测进行推理，缺乏对完整目录物品层的显式竞争建模，难以保证生成结果对应完整且合理的目录物品。

Method: EPIC在SID去噪过程中构建基于当前生成上下文和用户近期交互的、针对可行候选物品的个性化后验分布，并将该物品级后验分布反投影回未解析的SID位置，以引导后续token决策。方法保持预训练骨干冻结，无需额外的解码器前向传播。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上与强基线相比均取得一致性改进；诊断分析表明增益主要来源于保留去噪过程中有前景物品假设的个性化转移证据。

Conclusion: 显式的物品级后验条件化（将完整物品的竞争纳入token生成过程）是提升SID生成式推荐性能的有效途径，且无需重新训练或增加推理开销。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) generative recommendation predicts the next item by generating a short tuple of discrete tokens. Recent masked-diffusion methods improve this process through bidirectional context and flexible decoding, yet recommendation ultimately requires selecting among complete catalog items. At each denoising step, a partial SID can correspond to multiple feasible items, while existing methods primarily reason through position-wise token predictions. We propose Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning (EPIC), which introduces explicit item-level competition into SID denoising. EPIC constructs a personalized posterior over feasible candidate items using the current generation context and the user's recent interactions, then projects this distribution back to unresolved SID positions to guide subsequent token decisions. The pretrained backbone remains frozen and requires no additional decoder forward pass. Experiments on four Amazon benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines, while diagnostic analyses indicate that the gains primarily arise from personalized transition evidence that preserves promising item hypotheses during denoising.

</details>


### [161] [LLM4AIGQ: LLM-based AI Guidance Query Generation Framework for Multi Interest Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03674)
*Xiangchen Pan,Jiayi Xu,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Lingyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4AIGQ，一种基于大语言模型的AI引导查询生成方案，通过整合用户画像和历史交互序列对用户兴趣进行细分，推断每个子兴趣的消费意图并生成对应的引导查询，采用SFT、RL和DPO的后训练流程以及多级奖励设计来实现多目标优化，并通过nearline-generation和online-read架构满足时延约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI引导查询生成依赖两阶段"Q2AIGQ"关联范式，通过多路径检索召回用户主搜索查询后再用规则方法泛化生成。该方式存在语义漂移问题（信息级联损失），且主查询推导过度依赖"用户-物品"共现关系，缺乏对用户多兴趣的探索，导致生成的引导查询价值低且与购买意图不匹配。

Method: 提出LLM-based解决方案LLM4AIGQ，核心方法包括：(1)整合用户画像与历史交互序列对用户兴趣进行细分；(2)对每个子兴趣推断具体消费意图并生成对应AIGQ；(3)采用包含SFT、RL和DPO的分阶段后训练流水线；(4)设计多级奖励机制以满足多目标优化和长链推理需求；(5)部署上采用近线生成+在线读取架构以符合延迟要求。

Result: 大量实验（包括离线评估和在线A/B测试）表明该模型在性能上具有稳健表现。

Conclusion: LLM4AIGQ通过大语言模型替代传统基于共现检索的思路，有效解决了语义漂移和用户多兴趣探索不足的问题，在离线与在线场景中均验证了其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Guidance queries stimulate user consumption by extracting preferences to provide search queries with guidance value, playing a crucial role in the e-commerce field. Traditional AI-generated queries (AIGQ) generation primarily relies on a two-stage "Query-to-AI-Generated-Query" (Q2AIGQ) association paradigm, first recalling user primary search queries from user profiles, historical behavior sequences, item-side information, and the current query through multi-path retrieval, then generalizing AIGQ via rule-based methods. This approach suffers from semantic drift due to information cascade loss; additionally, primary search query derivation heavily depends on "user-item" co-occurrence relationships, lacking exploration of user multi-interests, resulting in guidance queries with low value and mismatched purchase intent. To address the expressive limitations of traditional co-occurrence-based retrieval, we propose LLM4AIGQ, an LLM-based solution for generating AI guidance queries tailored to users' multi-interests. This approach segments user interests by integrating user profiles and historical interaction sequences, infers specific consumption intents for each sub-interest, and subsequently generates corresponding AIGQ. In terms of model training, we employ a post-training pipeline comprising Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the model's capability in generating AIGQ. We also introduce a multi-level reward design to satisfy the requirements of multi-objective optimization and long-chain reasoning in practical applications. Regarding deployment, we adopt a nearline-generation and online-read architecture to meet latency constraints. Extensive experimental analyses demonstrate that our model achieves robust performance in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests.

</details>


### [162] [Unified Pitch Graphs for Diagnosing Pitching Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03810)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一投球图（UPG），一种用于棒球投球策略回顾性分析的分层图表示。UPG将每次投球保留为带重建三维轨迹和上下文信息的精确事件，通过有向序列边连接连续投球，并在不同语义和时间分辨率下组织相同事件。在2021-2026年394万MLB Statcast投球数据上评估，支持自适应回溯将已保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685，并能可靠定位离散投球组合和序列表示无法检测的受控执行变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒球投球策略既体现在物理执行层面，也体现在投球使用的有序上下文语境中，但现有常见表示方法将投球简化为离散类型或汇总统计量，丢失了精确的顺序时空信息和执行细节，导致无法识别细粒度策略模式。

Method: 提出Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG)分层图表示：1) 将每次投球保存为具有重建三维轨迹和上下文的精确事件节点；2) 用有向序列边连接连续投球；3) 在语义和时间分辨率上组织相同事件形成层次结构；4) 设计支持自适应（support-adaptive）回溯机制，当细粒度长序列缺乏重复证据时自动降级到粗略表示，同时保留精确事件谱系。

Result: 在394万MLB Statcast投球数据（2021-2026）上：名义上相同的投球序列展现出不同的物理执行方式；有序结构在更长上下文条件下的路径中越发明显；支持自适应回溯将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提高到94.9%，并将执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685；UPG能可靠定位离散投球组合和序列表示无法检测的受控执行变化。

Conclusion: UPG提供了一种可追溯、多尺度的表示方法，能够在不断然混淆回顾性关联与因果性或未来表现声明的前提下，识别反复出现的策略模式。

Abstract: Pitching strategy in baseball is expressed through both physical execution and the ordered context in which pitches are used, yet common representations collapse pitches into discrete types or aggregate statistics. We present Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG), a hierarchical graph representation for retrospective analysis of sequential spatiotemporal events. UPG preserves each pitch as an exact event with reconstructed three-dimensional trajectory and context, connects consecutive pitches through directed sequence edges, and organizes the same events across semantic and temporal resolutions. A support-adaptive mechanism backs off from fine, long sequences when repeated evidence is insufficient, while retaining exact event lineage. We evaluate UPG on 3.94 million MLB Statcast pitches from 2021 to 2026. Nominally identical pitch sequences exhibit distinct physical executions, and ordered structure becomes increasingly evident in longer context-conditioned paths. Support-adaptive backoff increases held-out path coverage from 18.9\% to 94.9\% while improving execution reconstruction from $R^2=0.495$ to $0.685$. UPG also reliably localizes controlled execution changes that discrete pitch-mix and sequence representations cannot detect. These results demonstrate that UPG provides a traceable, multi-scale representation for identifying recurring strategy patterns without conflating retrospective associations with causal or future-performance claims.

</details>


### [163] [GRASP: Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline for Label-Free Multi-Topic Essay Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03857)
*Aafreen Husain,Samar Shailendra,Saad Sajid Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRASP（Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline），一种用于自动批改无标签多主题科学简答题的流水线。该方法将各题参考答案编码为FAISS向量索引并构建语义相似图，通过句子计数启发式结合大语言模型判断学生段落中包含的主题数量，再结合余弦相似度的RAG与图遍历的GRAG检索候选参考答案节点，最后用匈牙利算法将参考答案与各段落片段进行最优匹配并独立评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动简答题评分研究聚焦于单一主题的考试，而对涉及多主题、且学生回答混合在单个段落中（无标签无分割）的考试自动评分仍缺乏探索。这类"无标签多主题"场景对检索与评分提出了新的挑战，需要一种无需训练数据或领域示例的自动化解决方案。

Method: GRASP流水线包含以下步骤：(1) 将每个问题的参考答案通过Sentence-BERT编码到FAISS向量索引中，构建参考答案语义相似图；(2) 在评分时，先通过句子计数启发式结合大语言模型判断学生段落中包含几个不同主题的回答；(3) 通过余弦相似度的RAG和基于图遍历的GRAG检索候选参考答案节点（GRAG以top余弦匹配为种子节点，沿强边进行图遍历以补全RAG遗漏的节点）；(4) 使用匈牙利算法将参考答案与各问题片段进行最优配对，避免重复；(5) 用GPT-4.1-mini对各片段独立评分。整个过程无需训练数据或领域示例。

Result: 该实验旨在展示检索质量对评分准确性的影响，以及在多种文章复杂度下，图增强检索相较纯余弦相似度方法的优势。

Conclusion: GRASP为无标签多主题科学简答题提供了一套无需训练数据与领域示例的端到端自动评分方案，并通过图增强检索弥补纯余弦检索的不足，提升了多主题场景下的检索与评分质量。

Abstract: Automated short-answer grading research has historically focused on exams consisting solely of questions pertaining to a single topic. Automatic grading of exams containing questions about more than one topic remains less explored. In this work, a Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline (GRASP) is introduced for grading label-free multi-topic science exams. Label-free exams are short-answer exams in which a student's responses to several distinct topics are merged into a single paragraph, with no markup labels or segmentation indicating which span answers which question. Reference answers for each question are encoded into a FAISS vector index via Sentence-BERT, and a semantic similarity graph is constructed over this set of reference answers. At grading time, sentence count heuristics, with a large language model used to resolve ambiguous cases, are first applied to predict how many distinct topics were answered in the student essay. This process is performed without training data or domain-specific example essays. Candidate reference nodes, each storing one (question, reference answer, concatenation of both) from the reference index, are then retrieved through cosine similarity based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GRAG). GRAG operates by taking the top cosine matches as seed nodes and then performing a graph traversal over strong edges to find additional reference nodes that may have been missed by RAG. The Hungarian algorithm is then used to optimally assign one reference node per question segment such that no reference is duplicated. Each segment is then graded against its assigned reference independently using GPT-4.1-mini. This experiment is performed to show the effect of retrieval quality on grading accuracy and the benefit of graph-augmented retrieval versus strict cosine similarity methods at various levels of essay complexity.

</details>


### [164] [Comparing Retrieval Methods for Academic Advisor Discovery: A Six-Method Study of 768 CS Faculty Profiles Across 9 US Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03901)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对六种信息检索方法（从稀疏词法匹配到稠密语义检索、混合与学习排序）在"学术导师发现"任务上进行了比较评估，基于新构建的数据集（768个CS教师简介、5个查询、162条相关性判断）。结果显示Reranked取得最高平均NDCG@10（0.477），TF-IDF显著最差；消融实验表明仅用传记字段优于完整模型，而直接拼接arXiv摘要会降低性能，支持后期融合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生申请者寻找匹配的学术导师是一个重要但研究不足的信息检索任务。作者旨在系统比较不同IR方法在该任务上的表现，并构建领域专属的评测数据集以填补缺乏标准评估资源的空白。

Method: 构建了包含768个来自9个美国CS系教师简介的新数据集，以及162条按0/1/2级的相关性判断。比较了六种方法：Jaccard重叠、TF-IDF、BM25、all-MiniLM-L6-v2句嵌入的稠密语义检索、混合分数融合和学习排序。进行了成对显著性检验（Bonferroni校正）、字段消融（传记vs研究标签）以及内容拼接对照实验（加入arXiv摘要）。

Result: Reranked在所有五个查询中取得最高平均NDCG@10（0.477），其次为Semantic（0.450）、Hybrid（0.421）、BM25（0.406）、Jaccard（0.303）和TF-IDF（0.246）。Bonferroni校正后仅TF-IDF显著差于BM25、Semantic、Hybrid和Reranked。仅传记字段（0.634）优于完整模型（0.593）；拼接arXiv摘要使NDCG@10降低0.176。

Conclusion: 学习排序方法在学术导师发现任务上表现最优，但前四名方法间无显著差异表明该任务尚需更强的检索信号。字段消融揭示"少即是多"——传记是最具判别力的单一信息源，直接拼接外部文本反而有害，建议采用后期融合架构。数据集和代码已开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: We present a comparative evaluation of six information retrieval methods for the task of academic advisor discovery: ranking CS faculty members by relevance to a graduate applicant's research interest statement. The methods span sparse lexical matching (Jaccard overlap, TF-IDF, BM25), dense semantic retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2 sentence embeddings), hybrid score fusion, and learning-to-rank. Evaluation uses a new domain-specific collection: 768 faculty profiles scraped from 9 US CS departments, with 162 graded relevance judgments (grade 0/1/2) across 5 queries representing distinct graduate student research profiles. Across all five queries, Reranked achieves the highest mean NDCG@10 (0.477, std 0.138), followed by Semantic (0.450), Hybrid (0.421), BM25 (0.406), Jaccard (0.303), and TF-IDF (0.246). After Bonferroni correction across all 15 pairwise comparisons, TF-IDF is significantly worse than BM25, Semantic, Hybrid, and Reranked; no other pairwise difference survives correction at 5 queries. A field ablation reveals that biography alone (NDCG 0.634) outperforms the full model combining biography with research area tags (0.593). A controlled experiment shows that concatenating arXiv paper abstracts reduces NDCG@10 by 0.176, motivating a late-fusion architecture. All code, scrapers, and relevance labels are released openly.

</details>


### [165] [The WebKurator.de Platform: Combined Regional and Topical Web Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03971)
*Michael Dinzinger,Natanael Arndt,Ben Böck,Jelena Mitrović,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出WebKurator.de，一个面向德国网络的两维（主题+地域）协作式网页策展平台。系统从德国印迹数据集（554万网站）中通过LLM进行主题分类并基于印迹页提取地址进行地理编码，成功为314万网站解析地址，其中258万（85.17%）位于德国且具备主题标签，构成平台初始基础，并通过用户建议持续扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家级图书馆和记忆机构在策展网络上具有文化/地域相关性的内容时面临挑战。现有基于目录的方法（如Curlie）采用以主题为中心的单一维度层级，将地理方面与主题和语言类别交织在一起，缺乏对区域性内容的有效分离和策展能力。

Method: WebKurator引入二维策展模型，明确分离主题分类与地理标注。系统集成基于LLM的主题分类和基于印迹（imprint）页面的地址提取（含地理编码），并支持用户建议及人工审核。平台从德国印迹数据集引导启动，覆盖554万网站。

Result: 从554万网站中，314万含印迹页面并成功提取和地理编码了邮政地址；其中258万（85.17%）位于德国并具有主题标签。这些网站构成WebKurator.de的初始基础。

Conclusion: WebKurator.de通过将地域与主题解耦的二维模型，结合自动化和人工审核机制，为德国网络提供了可扩展的区域性协作策展方案，且可通过用户建议持续扩展数据基础。

Abstract: The systematic curation of the Web remains a central challenge for national libraries and memory institutions that aim to preserve culturally and regionally relevant content. Existing directory-based approaches such as Curlie implement a predominantly topic-centric, one-dimensional hierarchy, where geographic aspects are intertwined with topical and linguistic categories. To address this limitation, we present WebKurator.de, a collaborative platform for combined regional and topical web curation, initially focused on the German web. WebKurator introduces a two-dimensional curation model that explicitly separates topical categorization and geographic annotation. The system integrates LLM-based topic classification and imprint-based address extraction with geocoding, and supports user suggestions together with moderated review. The platform is bootstrapped from the German Imprints Dataset, a large-scale collection of 5.54 million websites. Among them, 3.14 million contain imprint pages, for which we successfully extracted and geocoded postal addresses. Of these, 2.58 million (85.17%) are located in Germany and also have an assigned topic label. These websites form the initial foundation of WebKurator.de and can be continuously extended through user suggestions.

</details>


### [166] [The Dice Roll Method: A Standardized Protocol for Repeated-Query Auditing of Large Language Model Brand Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04047)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"掷骰子法"(Dice Roll Method)，一种用于审计大规模语言模型(LLM)品牌推荐中随机变异的标准化重复查询协议，包含统计框架、迭代次数指引和外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用重复相同提示来审计LLM品牌推荐中的随机变异，但缺乏标准化的协议来设定迭代次数、选择稳定性指标或建立可靠性阈值。

Method: 将总响应方差分解为采样、提示措辞、运行间和模型版本四个分量；采用负二项混合模型（迭代作为重复测量）、Cliff's delta作为无分布效应量、保持依赖性的bootstrap、基于仿真的功效分析、概化理论分解，以及固定快照上的漂移诊断。数据集包含5个品牌推荐审计研究、约19万观测、270+品牌、6种语言、迭代次数5至40。

Result: 从D-study得出三个迭代层级：探索性(n=5, G=0.58)、验证性(n=10, G=0.74)、严格性(n=15, G=0.81)；四种度量家族（计数、集合、嵌入、公平调整PASOR）互补。外部验证在39个单元中的37个复现了D-study可靠性预测且无失败，n=5的功效值精确到两位小数，但固定层级不具可迁移性，支持先导后解(pilot-then-solve)的解读。

Conclusion: 该协议在真实自回归生成的条件性、非高斯结构下，为LLM品牌推荐的重复查询审计提供了统计上严谨的基础。

Abstract: Background: Researchers increasingly use repeated identical prompts to audit stochastic variation in large language model (LLM) brand recommendations, yet no standardized protocol exists for setting iteration counts, selecting stability metrics, or establishing reliability thresholds. Objective: We formalize the Dice Roll Method as a reusable protocol for repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations, grounded in a generative model of temperature-scaled nucleus sampling. Methods: Total response variance is decomposed into sampling, prompt-phrasing, run-to-run, and model-version components. The stack: a negative-binomial mixed model with iterations as repeated measures; Cliff's delta as the distribution-free effect size; dependence-preserving bootstrap; simulation-based power; a generalizability-theory decomposition; drift diagnostics on pinned snapshots. We reanalyse five brand-recommendation auditing studies: approximately 190,000 observations, 270+ brands, 6 languages, iteration counts 5 to 40. Results: Three tiers of iteration guidance emerge from the D-study: exploratory (n = 5, G = 0.58), confirmatory (n = 10, G = 0.74), and rigorous (n = 15, G = 0.81), tied to effect-size and generalizability targets. The four metric families (count, set, embedding, fairness-adjusted PASOR) are complementary, motivating a compact metric battery over single indicators. A pre-registered external validation on three independent corpora (Motoki et al., 100-round; Rozado, 24 models; llm-stability) reproduces the D-study reliability prediction in 37 of 39 cells with no failures and the n = 5 power value to two decimals; the fixed tiers do not transfer, supporting a pilot-then-solve reading. Conclusion: The protocol gives repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations a statistically principled footing under the conditional, non-Gaussian structure of real autoregressive generation.

</details>
